				mux IMUX_D[2] = X1_S1[1] | X1_N1[1] | X2_S1[4] | X2_S1[5] | X2_S1[6] | X2_S1[7] | X2_S2[4] | X2_S2[5] | X2_S2[6] | X2_S2[7] | X2_N1[4] | X2_N1[5] | X2_N1[6] | X2_N1[7] | X2_N2[4] | X2_N2[5] | X2_N2[6] | X2_N2[7] | X6_S3[2] | X6_S3[3] | X6_S6[2] | X6_S6[3] | IMUX_D[1];
				mux PLC_IO[0] = OUT_F_PLC_IO[0] | OUT_Q_PLC_IO[0];
				mux PLC_IO[1] = OUT_F_PLC_IO[1] | OUT_Q_PLC_IO[1];

				output PADDI = OUT_F[2], OUT_IO_PADDI[2];
			}

			bel IO[3] {
				input PADDO = IMUX_IO_O[3];
				input PADDT = IMUX_IO_T[3];
				output PADDI = OUT_F[3], OUT_IO_PADDI[3];
			}

			bel IO[4] {
				input PADDO = IMUX_IO_O[4];
				input PADDT = IMUX_IO_T[4];
				output PADDI = OUT_F[4], OUT_IO_PADDI[4];
			}

			bel IO[5] {
				input PADDO = IMUX_IO_O[5];
				input PADDT = IMUX_IO_T[5];
				output PADDI = OUT_F[5], OUT_IO_PADDI[5];
			}

			switchbox IO_INT {
				mux IMUX_IO_O[0] = IMUX_A[0] | IMUX_B[0] | IMUX_IO_O_PLC[0];
				mux IMUX_IO_T[0] = IMUX_A[0] | IMUX_B[0];
				mux IMUX_IO_O[1] = IMUX_C[0] | IMUX_D[0] | IMUX_IO_O_PLC[1];
				mux IMUX_IO_T[1] = IMUX_C[0] | IMUX_D[0];
				mux IMUX_IO_O[2] = IMUX_A[1] | IMUX_B[1] | IMUX_IO_O_PLC[2];
				mux IMUX_IO_T[2] = IMUX_A[1] | IMUX_B[1];
				mux IMUX_IO_O[3] = IMUX_C[1] | IMUX_D[1] | IMUX_IO_O_PLC[3];
				mux IMUX_IO_T[3] = IMUX_C[1] | IMUX_D[1];
				mux IMUX_IO_O[4] = IMUX_A[2] | IMUX_B[2] | IMUX_IO_O_PLC[4];
				mux IMUX_IO_T[4] = IMUX_A[2] | IMUX_B[2];
				mux IMUX_IO_O[5] = IMUX_C[2] | IMUX_D[2] | IMUX_IO_O_PLC[5];
				mux IMUX_IO_T[5] = IMUX_C[2] | IMUX_D[2];
			}

			// wire IMUX_IO_O[5]                   IO[5].PADDO

			// wire IMUX_IO_T[2]                   IO[2].PADDT

		tile_class PLL_S {
			cell CELL;

			switchbox BEL_INT {
				mux IMUX_PLL_CLKI = IMUX_A[0] | IMUX_B[0] | IMUX_CLK[0] | IO_PLL_CLKI;
				mux IMUX_PLL_CLKFB = IMUX_C[0] | IMUX_CLK[1] | OUT_PLL_CLKINTFB | IO_PLL_CLKFB;
			}

			bel PLL[0] {
				input CLKI = IMUX_PLL_CLKI;
				input CLKFB = IMUX_PLL_CLKFB;
				input RESETM = IMUX_A[1];
				input RESETK = IMUX_B[1];
				input CNTRST = IMUX_D[0];
				input PWD = IMUX_C[1];
				input DDAMODE = IMUX_B[3];
				input DDAIZR = IMUX_A[2];
				input DDAILAG = IMUX_A[3];
				input DDAIDEL[0] = IMUX_D[2];
				input DDAIDEL[1] = IMUX_C[2];
				input DDAIDEL[2] = IMUX_B[2];
				input TCLKI = IMUX_D[1];
				output CLKOP = OUT_F[5], OUT_PLL_CLKOP;
				output CLKOS = OUT_F[6], OUT_PLL_CLKOS;
				output CLKOK = OUT_F[7], OUT_PLL_CLKOK;
				output CLKINTFB = OUT_PLL_CLKINTFB;
				output LOCK = OUT_OFX[0];
				output DNLOCK = OUT_OFX[3];
				output UPLOCK = OUT_OFX[2];
				output DDAOZR = OUT_F[0];
				output DDAOLAG = OUT_F[4];
				output DDAODEL[0] = OUT_F[3];
				output DDAODEL[1] = OUT_F[2];
				output DDAODEL[2] = OUT_F[1];
				output TESTOUT = OUT_OFX[1];
			}

			// wire IMUX_A[1]                      PLL[0].RESETM
